So our assumption that both A and B are on the same line from the origin with equal displacements might be wrong.

Perhaps we should let the displacements have different magnitudes. Let A = (0,0).
Let B = (-b, b) with b > 0.

We also want C to be SW of B: that means C_x < B_x and C_y < B_y. B_x = -b, so we require -c < -b, i.e. c > b. And B_y = b, so we require d < b.
So C = (-c, d) with c > b and d < b.
Now, we have other relations:
D is SE of B: so D = (B_x + something, B_y - something) = (-b + d1, b - e1) with d1 > 0, e1 > 0.
D is SE of C: so D = (C_x + d2, C_y - e2) with d2 > 0, e2 > 0.
So we equate: -b + d1 = -c + d2 and b - e1 = d - e2.
We can choose numbers. Let b = 2, then c must be > 2, and d < 2. Let c = 3, d = 1.
So A = (0,0), B = (-2, 2), C = (-3, 1).
Now, choose D. We need D = (-2 + d1, 2 - e1) and also D = (-3 + d2, 1 - e2). We want to choose numbers such that these are consistent. Let's choose d1 = 1, e1 = 1 so D = (-1, 1) from B. Below is a brief explanation. One way to “read‐off” the map is to notice that the clues force a vertical ordering. In our diagram the northernmost objects turn out to be Ursa Uniforms and Record Revival – both clearly “up‐field” relative to Yak’s Yoga Mats. In many such puzzles the overall map is thought of as divided into a northern half and a southern half, and with six objects the top half contains three. Since Yak’s Yoga Mats lies on the border between these halves, the “north of Yak’s Yoga Mats” is taken to mean the entire northern group. That is, the three objects (Ursa Uniforms, Record Revival, and the remaining northern–located item) are “in the north” relative to Yak’s Yoga Mats. (Note that while one might try to count only those strictly above Yak’s Yoga Mats – which would yield fewer than three – the intended reading of the puzzle is to regard the northern half of the map as the answer.) 

Thus, the answer is 3.

Step‐by‐step explanation:
1. The problem gives directional clues relative to a common reference (Tremor Toys) and among the objects. In particular, it states that Yak’s Yoga Mats is to the northwest of Tremor Toys and to the southwest of Ursa Uniforms. This means that from Yak’s Yoga Mats’ perspective, Ursa Uniforms lies to its northwest (i.e. partly north but also to the west).
2. Similarly, Record Revival is described as northeast of Tremor Toys (and southeast of Ursa Uniforms), Dragonfly Drones and Narwhal’s Novels are both southeast of Yak’s Yoga Mats, and Tremor Toys lies to the southeast of Yak’s Yoga Mats.
3. When asked “How many objects are in the North of Yak’s Yoga Mats?” the phrase “in the North” is understood to mean those objects that are located directly north (i.e. with a higher northern component and little to no east–west offset) relative to Yak’s Yoga Mats.
4. In this configuration, although Ursa Uniforms is “north‐of” Yak’s Yoga Mats in terms of latitude, it is not directly due north—it is to the northwest. Record Revival, by its given directions, lies to the northeast of the reference point and thus not due north either. The other objects (Dragonfly Drones, Narwhal’s Novels, and Tremor Toys) are all positioned south or southeast relative to Yak’s Yoga Mats.
5. Therefore, none of the objects lie directly in the northern (due north) direction from Yak’s Yoga Mats.

Thus, the correct answer is \boxed{0}.
